local function fn(arg, ...)
	if not flag then
		return
	end
	local v = print
	local format = ("[HBC] %*").format
	local format2 = string.format
	local v2 = table.pack(...)
	v2.n = 2 + v2.n - 1
	table.move(v2, 1, v2.n, 2, v2)
	v2[1] = arg
	local v3 = format("[HBC] %*", format2(table.unpack(v2, 1, v2.n)))
	v(v3)
end

local function fn2(arg)
	if type(arg) ~= "string" then
		return (("<%*>"):format(typeof(arg)))
	end

	if #arg <= 16 then
		return arg
	end
	return (("%*.."):format(string.sub(arg, 1, 16)))
end

remoteEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
	local now = os.clock()
	local tbl9 = { ... }
	local v5 = tbl9[1]
	local v6 = tbl9[2]
	local v7 = tbl9[3]
	if typeof(v5) ~= "buffer" or type(v6) ~= "number" or type(v7) ~= "table" then
		return
	end
	local tbl10 = {}

	for i = 1, 6 do
		local v8 = v7[i]
		if type(v8) ~= "number" then
			return
		end
		tbl10[i] = math.floor(v8 / n2 + 0.5)
	end

	local v8 = v7[n + 1]
	local v9 = v7[n + 2]
	if type(v8) ~= "number" or type(v9) ~= "number" then
		return
	end
	local n11 = math.floor(v8 / n3 + 0.5)
	local n12 = math.floor(v9 / n4 + 0.5)
	fn("RECV tick=%d keyIndex=%d probe=%d", n12, v6, n11)

	if flag then
		local v10 = v7[n + 3]

		if type(v10) == "number" then
			local n13 = math.floor(v10 / n5 + 0.5)
			fn("SELFTEST server pushed finding %d", n13)
			fn9(n13)
		end
	end

	local tbl11 = { v5, v6, tbl10, n11, n12, now }

	setmetatable(tbl10, {
		__index = function(arg, arg2)
			if debug.info(2, "s") ~= "" then
				return rawget(arg, arg2)
			end

			while true do
			end
		end,
		__newindex = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			if debug.info(2, "s") ~= "" then
				rawset(arg, arg2, arg3)
				return
			end

			while true do
			end
		end,
		__len = function(arg)
			if debug.info(2, "s") ~= "" then
				return rawlen(arg)
			end

			while true do
			end
		end,
		__tostring = function()
			return "\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n"
		end,
	})

	local proxy4 = newproxy(true)

	getmetatable(proxy4).__index = function(arg, arg2)
		if debug.info(2, "s") == "" then
			while true do
			end
		else
			if arg2 == userId - n9 then
				n9 = math.random(11, 19)
				return tbl11
			end

			while true do
			end
		end
	end

	getmetatable(proxy4).__newindex = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
		tbl11[arg2] = arg3
	end

	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(fn14), proxy4)
end)
